---
title: Domain rules
description: The decisions the domain makes, its status machines and what each change refuses, read from the code.
sidebar:
  order: 2
---

This page is written by `ianua-audit rules --markdown` from the domain's code, and a test fails when it falls behind. Do not edit it by hand.

Each table says what the code says, with the file and line it says it in. A row marked **not read** rests on code the reader could not work out; read the code there.

- **Decisions** are the domain's functions whose answer is chosen by a `match` over enums. One whose inputs are all enums is worked out for every combination, and **any** means the answer is the same whatever that input is. One that decides on values it works out itself is shown arm by arm, as written.
- **Status machines** list the states, the moves the machine allows, the states no move leaves, and the changes that move a value into each state, with the use cases that call them and the right each takes.
- **Refusals** list, for each change, what leads it to refuse and with which error.

## Audit

### Decisions

#### `AuditScope::of`

The scope an organization and a tenant give: the tenant when one is known, else the organization, else the platform.

Its last `match`, arm by arm, as written, at `domains/audit/record.rs:31`.

| `tenant` | `organization` | Answer (`AuditScope`) | Where |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Fact::Known(tenant)` | any | `Self::Tenant { organization, tenant, }` | `domains/audit/record.rs:33` |
| `Fact::Unknown` | `Fact::Known(organization)` | `Self::Organization(organization)` | `domains/audit/record.rs:37` |
| `Fact::Unknown` | `Fact::Unknown` | `Platform` | `domains/audit/record.rs:38` |

#### `StreamDestination::kind`

What kind of destination this is, which records may name.

Its last `match`, arm by arm, as written, at `domains/audit/streaming.rs:370`.

| `self` | Answer (`StreamDestinationKind`) | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Self::Splunk(_)` | `Splunk` | `domains/audit/streaming.rs:372` |
| `Self::Datadog(_)` | `Datadog` | `domains/audit/streaming.rs:373` |
| `Self::Syslog(_)` | `Syslog` | `domains/audit/streaming.rs:374` |
| `Self::Http(_)` | `Http` | `domains/audit/streaming.rs:375` |

### Status machines

#### `AuditExportStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/audit/export/state.rs:42`.

- States: `Pending`, `Ready`, `Failed`, `Expired`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Failed`, `Expired`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Pending` to `Failed` | `AuditLogExport::give_up` | ExpireUseCase (audit) | none read |
| `Pending` to `Ready` | none read | none read | none read |
| `Ready` to `Expired` | `AuditLogExport::expire` | ExpireUseCase (audit) | none read |

### Refusals

#### `AuditLogExport::expire`

| When | Refused with | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `self.state.downloadable_at(now)` | InvalidTransition | `domains/audit/export/mod.rs:269` |
| `self.status()` may not move to `to.status()` | InvalidTransition | `domains/audit/export/mod.rs:318`, through `move_to` |

#### `AuditLogExport::give_up`

| When | Refused with | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `now < self.requested_at.saturating_add(WRITE_DEADLINE)` | InvalidTransition | `domains/audit/export/mod.rs:252` |

#### `AuditLogExport::hand_out`

| When | Refused with | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `access.log() != self.log` | Forbidden | `domains/audit/export/mod.rs:296` |
| `self.state` is not `AuditExportState::Ready { rows,..}` | Conflict | `domains/audit/export/mod.rs:302` |
| `!self.state.downloadable_at(now)` | Conflict | `domains/audit/export/mod.rs:308` |

#### `AuditLogExport::mark_written`

| When | Refused with | Where |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `file.seal.is_some() != is_sealed(self.log, ready)` | Validation | `domains/audit/export/mod.rs:233` |
| `self.status()` may not move to `to.status()` | InvalidTransition | `domains/audit/export/mod.rs:318`, through `move_to` |

- `signal_asks` is `self.signal_fired(ctx) && !current.satisfies(self.asked_aal(ctx))`
- `policy_asks` is `!current.satisfies(self.required_aal(ctx))`

| `signal_asks` | `policy_asks` | `ctx.device` | Answer (`Demand`) | Where |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `true` | `true` | any | `SignalAndPolicy` | `domains/authentication/risk_engine/step_up.rs:455` |
| `true` | `false` | any | `Signal` | `domains/authentication/risk_engine/step_up.rs:456` |
| `false` | `true` | any | `Policy` | `domains/authentication/risk_engine/step_up.rs:457` |
| `false` | `false` | `Familiarity::New` | `OwnFactor` | `domains/authentication/risk_engine/step_up.rs:458` |
| `false` | `false` | `Familiarity::Known` | `NotAsked` | `domains/authentication/risk_engine/step_up.rs:459` |

#### `under_high_pressure`

The step `signals` lead to while sign-in is under high pressure.

Its last `match`, arm by arm, as written, at `domains/authentication/risk_engine/challenge.rs:130`.

| `signals.device` | `signals.setup` | `signals.proof` | Answer (`GateStep`) | Where |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Familiarity::Known` | any | any | `Proceed` | `domains/authentication/risk_engine/challenge.rs:132` |
| `Familiarity::New` | `ChallengeSetup::Unconfigured` | any | `GateStep::Refuse(RequiredChallenge::Unavailable)` | `domains/authentication/risk_engine/challenge.rs:133` |
| `Familiarity::New` | `ChallengeSetup::Configured(kind)` | `None` | `GateStep::Refuse(RequiredChallenge::Solve(kind))` | `domains/authentication/risk_engine/challenge.rs:136` |
| `Familiarity::New` | `ChallengeSetup::Configured(kind)` | `Some(proof)` | `GateStep::CheckProof(kind, proof)` | `domains/authentication/risk_engine/challenge.rs:139` |

### Status machines

#### `DeviceStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/authentication/device_status.rs:34`.

- States: `Trusted`, `Revoked`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Revoked`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Trusted` to `Revoked` | `TrustedDevice::revoke` | RevokeOrgDeviceUseCase (organization) | `AccountKeeper`, `AccountAction::ChangeSignIn` |

### Decisions

#### `PolicyCondition::check`

Checks the condition can mean what it says.

| `self` | Answer (`ValidationError`) | Where |

### Status machines

#### `AccessRequestStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/authorization/access_request.rs:155`.

- States: `Pending`, `Approved`, `Denied`, `Cancelled`, `Expired`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Approved`, `Denied`, `Cancelled`, `Expired`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Pending` to `Approved` | `AccessRequest::approve` | ApproveUseCase (authorization) | `TenantAuthority`, `SystemPermission::Roles(RoleAction::Assign)` |
| `Pending` to `Cancelled` | `AccessRequest::cancel` | CancelUseCase (authorization) | `TenantAuthority` |
| `Pending` to `Denied` | `AccessRequest::deny` | DenyUseCase (authorization) | `TenantAuthority`, `SystemPermission::Roles(RoleAction::Assign)` |
| `Pending` to `Expired` | `AccessRequest::expire` | ExpireUseCase (authorization) | none read |

#### `AccessReviewStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/authorization/access_review.rs:119`.

- States: `Open`, `Completed`, `Cancelled`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Completed`, `Cancelled`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Open` to `Cancelled` | `AccessReview::cancel` | CancelUseCase (authorization) | `TenantAuthority` |
| `Open` to `Completed` | `AccessReview::finish` | CompleteOverdueUseCase (authorization), CompleteUseCase (authorization) | `ReviewCompletion` |

#### `PolicyStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/authorization/policy/status.rs:54`.

- States: `Draft`, `Review`, `Active`, `Archived`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Archived`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Active` to `Archived` | `PolicySet::archive` | ChangeStatusUseCase (authorization) | `TenantAuthority` |
| `Draft` to `Archived` | `PolicySet::archive` | ChangeStatusUseCase (authorization) | `TenantAuthority` |
| `Draft` to `Review` | `PolicySet::submit_for_review` | none read | `TenantAuthority` |
| `Review` to `Active` | `PolicySet::activate` | ChangeStatusUseCase (authorization) | `PolicyApprover`, `SystemPermission::PolicySet(PolicySetAction::Activate)` |
| `Review` to `Draft` | `PolicySet::start_draft`, `PolicySet::reject` | StartDraftUseCase (authorization) | `TenantAuthority`, `SystemPermission::PolicySet(PolicySetAction::Update)` |

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

### Status machines

#### `ErasureStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/identity/privacy/erasure_status.rs:56`.

- States: `Pending`, `Processing`, `Completed`, `Cancelled`, `Held`, `Failed`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Completed`, `Cancelled`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Failed` to `Processing` | `ErasureRequest::start_processing`, `ErasureRequest::retry`, `OrgErasureRequest::start_processing`, `OrgErasureRequest::retry` | ProcessErasuresUseCase (identity), ProcessOrgErasuresUseCase (organization) | none read |
| `Held` to `Cancelled` | `ErasureRequest::cancel_from_link`, `ErasureRequest::cancel_for`, `OrgErasureRequest::cancel_from_link`, `OrgErasureRequest::cancel_for` | CancelErasureUseCase (identity), CancelOrgErasureByLinkUseCase (organization), CancelOrgErasureUseCase (organization) | `AccountControlCheck`, `AccountAction::Erase`, `OrgAccountControl` |
| `Held` to `Pending` | `ErasureRequest::submit`, `ErasureRequest::submit_for`, `ErasureRequest::release`, `OrgErasureRequest::submit`, `OrgErasureRequest::submit_for`, `OrgErasureRequest::release` | RequestErasureUseCase (identity), HoldErasureUseCase (identity), ReleaseErasureUseCase (identity), RequestOrgErasureUseCase (organization), HoldOrgErasureUseCase (organization), ReleaseOrgErasureUseCase (organization) | `SignedInPerson`, `AccountControlCheck`, `AccountAction::Erase`, `OrgControl`, `OrgAccountControl` |
| `Pending` to `Cancelled` | `ErasureRequest::cancel_from_link`, `ErasureRequest::cancel_for`, `OrgErasureRequest::cancel_from_link`, `OrgErasureRequest::cancel_for` | CancelErasureUseCase (identity), CancelOrgErasureByLinkUseCase (organization), CancelOrgErasureUseCase (organization) | `AccountControlCheck`, `AccountAction::Erase`, `OrgAccountControl` |
| `Pending` to `Held` | `ErasureRequest::hold`, `OrgErasureRequest::hold` | HoldErasureUseCase (identity), ReleaseErasureUseCase (identity), HoldOrgErasureUseCase (organization), ReleaseOrgErasureUseCase (organization) | `AccountControlCheck`, `AccountAction::Erase`, `OrgAccountControl` |
| `Pending` to `Processing` | `ErasureRequest::start_processing`, `ErasureRequest::retry`, `OrgErasureRequest::start_processing`, `OrgErasureRequest::retry` | ProcessErasuresUseCase (identity), ProcessOrgErasuresUseCase (organization) | none read |
| `Processing` to `Completed` | `ErasureRequest::complete`, `OrgErasureRequest::complete` | ProcessErasuresUseCase (identity), ProcessOrgErasuresUseCase (organization) | none read |
| `Processing` to `Failed` | `ErasureRequest::fail`, `OrgErasureRequest::fail` | ProcessErasuresUseCase (identity), ProcessOrgErasuresUseCase (organization) | none read |

### Status machines

#### `TopicStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/messaging/topic.rs:66`.

- States: `Active`, `Paused`, `Archived`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Archived`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Active` to `Archived` | `Topic::archive` | none read | `MessagingControl`, `MessagingAction::Delete` |
| `Active` to `Paused` | `Topic::pause` | none read | `MessagingControl`, `MessagingAction::Manage` |
| `Paused` to `Active` | `Topic::create`, `Topic::resume` | CreateUseCase (messaging) | `MessagingCreator`, `MessagingControl`, `MessagingAction::Manage` |
| `Paused` to `Archived` | `Topic::archive` | none read | `MessagingControl`, `MessagingAction::Delete` |

#### `DeviceAuthStatus`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Approved` to `Redeemed` | `DeviceAuthorization::redeem` | CollectDeviceTokensUseCase (oauth) | none read |
| `Pending` to `Approved` | `DeviceAuthorization::approve` | ApproveDeviceUseCase (oauth) | `SignedInPerson` |
| `Pending` to `Denied` | `DeviceAuthorization::deny` | DenyDeviceUseCase (oauth) | `SignedInPerson` |
| `Pending` to `Expired` | `DeviceAuthorization::expire` | ExpireDeviceAuthorizationsUseCase (oauth) | none read |

| `Active` to `Revoked` | `OrgApiKey::revoke` | RevokeOrgUseCase (organization) | `ActedBy`, `OrgControl`, `OrgPermission::ApiKeys(OrgApiKeyAction::Revoke)` |

#### `OrgUserStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/organization/members/org_user_status.rs:115`.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `Active` to `Deactivated` | none read | none read | none read |
| `Active` to `Locked` | none read | none read | none read |
| `Closing` to `Active` | `OrgUser::verify_email`, `OrgUser::confirm_as_installer`, `OrgUser::accept_invitation` | ConfirmSignupUseCase (onboarding), FoundAnotherOrganizationUseCase (onboarding), InstallUseCase (onboarding), AcceptOrgUseCase (organization) | `VerifiedEmail`, `InstallerProof`, `AcceptedAddress` |
| `Closing` to `Deactivated` | none read | none read | none read |
| `Deactivated` to `Active` | `OrgUser::verify_email`, `OrgUser::confirm_as_installer`, `OrgUser::accept_invitation` | ConfirmSignupUseCase (onboarding), FoundAnotherOrganizationUseCase (onboarding), InstallUseCase (onboarding), AcceptOrgUseCase (organization) | `VerifiedEmail`, `InstallerProof`, `AcceptedAddress` |
| `Deactivated` to `Invited` | `OrgUser::reopen_for_invitation` | InviteOrgUserUseCase (organization) | `OrgControl`, `MemberAction::Invite`, `SystemPermission::Roles(RoleAction::Escalate)` |
| `Invited` to `Active` | `OrgUser::verify_email`, `OrgUser::confirm_as_installer`, `OrgUser::accept_invitation` | ConfirmSignupUseCase (onboarding), FoundAnotherOrganizationUseCase (onboarding), InstallUseCase (onboarding), AcceptOrgUseCase (organization) | `VerifiedEmail`, `InstallerProof`, `AcceptedAddress` |
| `Invited` to `Deactivated` | none read | none read | none read |
| `Locked` to `Active` | `OrgUser::verify_email`, `OrgUser::confirm_as_installer`, `OrgUser::accept_invitation` | ConfirmSignupUseCase (onboarding), FoundAnotherOrganizationUseCase (onboarding), InstallUseCase (onboarding), AcceptOrgUseCase (organization) | `VerifiedEmail`, `InstallerProof`, `AcceptedAddress` |
| `Locked` to `Deactivated` | none read | none read | none read |
| `PendingVerification` to `Active` | `OrgUser::verify_email`, `OrgUser::confirm_as_installer`, `OrgUser::accept_invitation` | ConfirmSignupUseCase (onboarding), FoundAnotherOrganizationUseCase (onboarding), InstallUseCase (onboarding), AcceptOrgUseCase (organization) | `VerifiedEmail`, `InstallerProof`, `AcceptedAddress` |
| `PendingVerification` to `Deactivated` | none read | none read | none read |

#### `OrganizationStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/organization/status.rs:39`.

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `Suspended` to `Closed` | none read | none read | none read |

### Status machines

#### `LogTargetStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/proxy/log_shipping/mod.rs:53`.

- States: `Active`, `Paused`
- Final, which no move leaves: none

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Active` to `Paused` | `LogShippingTarget::pause` | none read | `RoutingControl`, `RoutingRight::Update` |
| `Paused` to `Active` | `LogShippingTarget::create`, `LogShippingTarget::resume` | CreateLogTargetUseCase (proxy) | `RoutingControl`, `RoutingRight::Create`, `RoutingRight::Update` |

#### `PipelineStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/proxy/pipeline_status.rs:40`.

- States: `Draft`, `Active`, `Disabled`
- Final, which no move leaves: none

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Active` to `Disabled` | `Pipeline::disable` | ImportConfigUseCase (gateway) | `RoutingControl`, `RoutingRight::ChangePipeline` |
| `Disabled` to `Active` | `Pipeline::activate` | ImportConfigUseCase (gateway) | `RoutingControl`, `RoutingRight::ChangePipeline` |
| `Disabled` to `Draft` | `Pipeline::with_defaults` | ConfigureUseCase (proxy), ImportConfigUseCase (gateway) | `RoutingControl`, `RoutingRight::CreatePipeline` |
| `Draft` to `Active` | `Pipeline::activate` | ImportConfigUseCase (gateway) | `RoutingControl`, `RoutingRight::ChangePipeline` |

### Status machines

#### `ConnectorStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/routing/connector/mod.rs:63`.

- States: `Active`, `Revoked`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Revoked`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Active` to `Revoked` | `PrivateConnector::revoke` | RevokeUseCase (routing) | `RoutingControl`, `RoutingRight::Update` |

#### `RouteStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/routing/route_status.rs:33`.

- States: `Active`, `Disabled`
- Final, which no move leaves: none

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Active` to `Disabled` | `Route::disable`, `Service::disable`, `StreamRoute::disable` | DisableUseCase (routing), ImportConfigUseCase (gateway), SetStatusUseCase (routing) | `RoutingControl`, `RoutingRight::Update` |
| `Disabled` to `Active` | `Route::create`, `Route::activate`, `Service::create`, `Service::activate`, `StreamRoute::create`, `StreamRoute::activate` | CreateUseCase (routing), ImportConfigUseCase (gateway), ActivateUseCase (routing), SetStatusUseCase (routing) | `RoutingControl`, `RoutingRight::Create`, `RoutingRight::Update` |

| `Pending` to `Running` | `ScheduledJob::mark_running` | none read | none read |
| `Running` to `Completed` | `ScheduledJob::mark_completed` | ProcessJobsUseCase (scheduling) | none read |
| `Running` to `Failed` | `ScheduledJob::mark_failed`, `ScheduledJob::abandon` | ProcessJobsUseCase (scheduling) | none read |

### Status machines

#### `AccountStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/workflows/account/mod.rs:59`.

- States: `Active`, `Disabled`
- Final, which no move leaves: none

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Active` to `Disabled` | `IntegrationAccount::disable` | ChangeUseCase (workflows) | `WorkflowControl`, `WorkflowAction::ConnectAccounts` |
| `Disabled` to `Active` | `IntegrationAccount::connect`, `IntegrationAccount::enable` | ConnectUseCase (workflows), FinishUseCase (workflows), ChangeUseCase (workflows) | `WorkflowControl`, `WorkflowAction::ConnectAccounts` |

#### `ExecutionStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/workflows/execution/mod.rs:88`.

- States: `Running`, `Ended`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Ended`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Running` to `Ended` | `WorkflowExecution::cancel`, `WorkflowExecution::expire`, `WorkflowExecution::stop_pass` | CancelUseCase (workflows), DriveUseCase (workflows), StopPassUseCase (workflows) | `WorkflowControl`, `WorkflowAction::Manage` |

#### `MemoryStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/workflows/memory/mod.rs:78`.

- States: `Active`, `DeletionRequested`
- Final, which no move leaves: none

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Active` to `DeletionRequested` | `Memory::request_deletion` | DeleteUseCase (workflows) | `WorkflowControl`, `WorkflowAction::Delete` |
| `DeletionRequested` to `Active` | `Memory::create`, `Memory::restore` | CreateUseCase (workflows), RestoreUseCase (workflows) | `WorkflowControl`, `WorkflowAction::Create`, `WorkflowAction::Delete` |

#### `SandboxStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/workflows/sandbox/mod.rs:68`.

- States: `Active`, `Archived`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Archived`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Active` to `Archived` | `Sandbox::archive` | ArchiveSandboxUseCase (workflows) | `WorkflowControl`, `WorkflowAction::Delete` |

#### `WorkflowStatus`

Its moves are read from `try_transition`, at `domains/workflows/design/workflow/version.rs:35`.

- States: `Draft`, `Published`, `Archived`
- Final, which no move leaves: `Archived`

| Move | Made by | Called by | Proof or right |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Draft` to `Archived` | `Workflow::archive` | ArchiveWorkflowUseCase (workflows) | `WorkflowControl`, `WorkflowAction::Delete` |
| `Draft` to `Published` | `Workflow::flow`, `Workflow::publish` | CreateUseCase (workflows) | `WorkflowControl`, `WorkflowAction::Create`, `WorkflowAction::Run`, `WorkflowAction::Update` |
| `Published` to `Archived` | `Workflow::archive` | ArchiveWorkflowUseCase (workflows) | `WorkflowControl`, `WorkflowAction::Delete` |
